a SmartRider Free Transit Zone for trains.\n\n### [Free Transit Zone for buses](https://www.transperth.wa.gov.au/tickets-fares/free-travel#)\n- To travel free, you must start and finish your journey within the zone\n- If your journey starts or finishes outside the zone, you must pay a fare for the whole journey\n- The Free Transit Zone logo on bus stops identifies the boundaries of the zone\n- If your journey either starts or finishes outside the zone, you should tag on and tag off in the normal way\n- If you tag on inside the zone (with your SmartRider or contactless payment method) without use a SmartRider or contactless payment method, start and finish your journey within the zone and tag on and off in the normal way\n- The boundaries of the zone are City West Station, Elizabeth Quay Station and Claisebrook Station.\n\n## Every Sunday Free\n![Every Sunday Free         "url": "https://www.virginaustralia.com/au/en/destinations/perth/getting-around-perth/",
        "title": "Getting around Perth - Your complete transport guide - Virgin Australia",
        "description": "# Getting around Perth –Your guide to public transport and more\n![Ferry in Elizabeth Quay Marina at sunset with skyscrapers of Central Business District on background in Perth, Western Australia.  ](https://www.virginaustralia.com/content/dam/vaa/img/destinations/perth/getting-around/Getting-around-perth-main.jpg/_jcr_content/renditions/vaacore.web.720.0.jpg)\n\nCredit: bennymarty from stock.adobe.com\n\n![Christelle Delli](https://www.virginaustralia.com/content/dam/vaa/img/author/christelle-delli.jpg)\n\nChristelle Delli\n\nPublished 28 January 2025\n\nFrom scenic ferries on the Swan River to free inner-city trains and buses, getting around Perth is easy and convenient.\n\n[Perth](https://www.virginaustralia.com/au/en/destinations/perth/) is a coastal paradise– its endless blue skies, stunning beaches, and buzzing cultural scene make it a must-visit destination.\n\nBut with so much to see, planning your [Perth itinerary](https://www.virginaustralia.com/au/en/destinations/perth/things-to-do-in-perth/perth-itinerary/) can be tricky.\n\nLuckily, getting around Perth is easy, with plenty of transport options available.\n\nWhile there's always the option to drive or catch a taxi or rideshare, Perth offers an excellent and comprehensive public transport network, that includes trains, buses, and ferries, making it easy to get around without hassle.\n\nIn this guide, we’ll walk you through all the transport options available to get you from one amazing spot to the next and make your trip seamless and comfortable.\n\nFlights to Perth from $309*\n\neconomy one way, lite fare\n\nfrom Sydney\n\nFind dates\n\n*Price is in AUD per adult, based on booking at virginaustralia.com. Subject to availability. Seat selection and checked baggage are not included in Economy Lite fares. Travel periods may apply. T&Cs apply.\n\n## Arriving in Perth\nVirgin Australia [flights to Perth](https://www.virginaustralia.com/en-au/flights-to-perth) from major Australian cities like [Sydney](https://www.virginaustralia.com/en-au/flights-from-sydney-to-perth), [Melbourne](https://www.virginaustralia.com/en-au/flights-from-melbourne-to-perth), or [Brisbane](https://www.virginaustralia.com/en-au/flights-from-brisbane-to-perth) land at Terminal 1 or 2, while international flights arrive in Terminal 1, 3 or 4.\n\n### Getting from Perth Airport to Perth CBD\n- **Taxis:** A taxi ride to the CBD costs approximately $45–$60, depending on traffic conditions and your exact destination. Note that your driver will add the $4 airport fee that applies to all pick-ups at Perth Airport to your fare. Taxi ranks are available at the front of all terminals – look for signs directing you to the nearest one. There's also a taxi rank on Valentine Road in the General Aviation area.\n- **Rideshares:** If you prefer a rideshare, options like Ubre or DiDi are available at Perth Airport. Every terminal has a designated rideshare pick-up area. The price varies between $30 and $60 depending on your destination, the time of the day and how busy it is when you order. As with taxi rides, a $4 airport fee will be applied to your fare.\n- **Airport shuttles:** A few private companies offer shuttle bus services from Perth Airport. [The Perth Airport Shuttle](https://www.perthairportshuttle.com.au/) runs between the airport and city hotels and costs from $50 per person. This door-to-door service runs 24/7 and if you travel with a young family, you also have the option to request a baby seat. You can either meet your chauffeur at a designated pick-up point or request the meet-and-greet service where your chauffeur will hold a sign with your name. Alternatively, we’d recommend that you contact your hotel to see whether they have a shuttle bus service as it could be cheaper.\n- **Public transport:** Perth Airport is well connected to the city, making it easy to reach your destination using public transport. From the T1 and T2 terminals, you can catch a train from Airport Central Station to Perth Station in the CBD. The trip takes 18 minutes and costs $5. If you prefer to catch the bus, Bus Route 37 operates between Airport Central and Oats Street Station Monday to Friday.\n- **Car rentals:** Renting a car allows for more flexibility for your time in Perth. You can either hire a car before your trip to earn Velocity Points, or head to one of the major car rental agencies including Avis and Hertz, located at Perth Airport.\n\n## How to get around Perth\n### Public transport\nPerth’s transport network, managed by Transperth, includes buses, trains, ferries, and free transit zones to simplify travel. In Perth, you can travel anywhere on the Transperth network, up to nine zones, for no more than a two-zone fare!\n\n### Taxis and ridesharing\nWhether you're heading to your accommodation in Perth, the city’s attractions, the airport, or just need a quick ride across town, taxis and rideshare services are readily available in Perth.\n\nPerth’s taxis are metered, and you can either flag one down or book through a taxi app. Well-known companies like [13Cabs](https://www.13cabs.com.au/locations/perth/) and [Universal Cabs](https://universalcabs.com.au/) operate across the city, and their websites allow for quick bookings. [13Cabs](https://www.13cabs.com.au/locations/perth/) even has an app and provides an estimate of your fare. You’ll also find designated taxi ranks at popular locations such as shopping centres, Perth Airport, and major transport hubs.\n\nIf you prefer ridesharing, both Uber and DiDi are a convenient way of getting around Perth, offering a seamless and flexible transport option. Shebah is another rideshare service available in Perth. This all-female fleet is a great choice for women and children, ensuring a safe and comfortable journey.\n\n### Accessible transport options\nMost of the city’s buses, trains, and ferries are designed to be accessible, with ramps and spaces for passengers who need extra assistance.\n\nAdditionally, Perth’s trains, including those operating on the Armadale, Fremantle, and Joondalup lines, are all wheelchair-friendly, ensuring a smooth journey across the city. However not all train stations provide suitable access to people with reduced mobility. Transperth Map allows travellers to identify stations listed as “ [Independent Access”](https://www.transperth.wa.gov.au/Using-Transperth/Station-Facilities/Stations-Maps). These stations allow the majority of passengers to access the station independently either via ramps, pathways or lifts.\n\nWhen it comes to taxis, wheelchair-accessible services are available through providers like 13Cabs, which can be booked in advance to ensure a comfortable ride. Perth Airport is also fully equipped with accessible facilities, offering ramps, elevators, and mobility services to assist passengers with reduced mobility.\n\n## Perth transport FAQs\nThe best way to get around Perth depends on what you’re planning to do. If you’re sticking to the city centre and nearby attractions, the public transport system is a convenient and affordable option. Perth's train and bus network, operated by Transperth, covers a wide area, with the city’s Free Transit Zone making it easy to travel around the CBD at no cost. It’s also incredibly easy to get around the city by foot, so if you’re sticking to the city centre, there’s no need to have a car.\n\nFor those looking to explore beyond Perth’s centre, like the beautiful Swan River, Fremantle or Cottesloe Beach, trains or buses are quick and reliable. Alternatively, taxis and rideshare services like Uber are readily available for a more direct journey.\n\nAbsolutely, it’s very easy to get around Perth without a car! Perth has an extensive and efficient public transport network that makes getting around the city and its suburbs easy without a car. Trains, buses, and ferries connect key locations with regular services throughout the day. The CBD is especially walkable, and many of the top [Perth attractions](https://www.virginaustralia.com/au/en/destinations/perth/things-to-do-in-perth/perth-attractions/) are within walking distance or accessible by public transport. Additionally, rideshare options like Uber and DiDi are always available. Many visitors find that they don’t need a car when staying in the city centre.\n\n## Flights to Perth\nVirgin Australia offers direct flights to Perth from major Australian cities, including:",

Whether you’re heading to iconic beaches or exploring vibrant neighbourhoods, this guide covers all your transport options.\n\n[Perth](https://www.virginaustralia.com/au/en/destinations/perth/) is a coastal paradise– its endless blue skies, stunning beaches, and buzzing cultural scene make it a must-visit destination.\n\nBut with so much to see, planning your [Perth itinerary](https://www.virginaustralia.com/au/en/destinations/perth/things-to-do-in-perth/perth-itinerary/) can be tricky. Whether you want to wander through vibrant laneways in Northbridge, explore the natural beauty of Kings Park, or soak up the sun at Cottesloe Beach, knowing how to get to Perth’s attractions is key to making the most of your visit – and removing the stress of being in a new city!\n\nLuckily, getting around Perth is easy, with plenty of transport options available.\n\nWhile there's always the option to drive or catch a taxi or rideshare, Perth offers an excellent and comprehensive public transport network, that includes trains, buses, and ferries, making it easy to get around without hassle.\n\nIn this guide, we’ll walk you through all the transport options available to get you from one amazing spot to the next and make your trip seamless and comfortable.\n\nFlights to Perth from $309\\*\n\neconomy one way, lite fare\n\nfrom Sydney\n\nFind dates\n\n\\*Price is in AUD per adult, based on booking at virginaustralia.com. Subject to availability. Seat selection and checked baggage are not included in Economy Lite fares. Travel periods may apply. T&Cs apply.\n\n## Arriving in Perth\n\nVirgin Australia [flights to Perth](https://www.virginaustralia.com/en-au/flights-to-perth) from major Australian cities like [Sydney](https://www.virginaustralia.com/en-au/flights-from-sydney-to-perth), [Melbourne](https://www.virginaustralia.com/en-au/flights-from-melbourne-to-perth), or [Brisbane](https://www.virginaustralia.com/en-au/flights-from-brisbane-to-perth) land at Terminal 1 or 2, while international flights arrive in Terminal 1, 3 or 4.\n\n### Getting from Perth Airport to Perth CBD\n\n![Perth airport taxi rank ](https://www.virginaustralia.com/content/dam/vaa/img/destinations/perth/getting-around/perth-airport-taxi-rank.jpg/_jcr_content/renditions/vaacore.web.720.0.jpg)\n\nCredit: beeboys from stock.adobe.com\n\n[Perth Airport](https://www.virginaustralia.com/au/en/travel-info/at-the-airport/guides/australia/western-australia/perth/) is conveniently located 17km from Perth CBD, which is roughly a 20-minute drive, depending on traffic.\n\nThere are a few different options to get into the city from the airport, but the quickest and most convenient way is by using taxi or rideshare. For a more budget-friendly option, the airport is seamlessly connected to the city via public transport.\n\nWe’ve listed below all of your options.\n\n- **Taxis:** A taxi ride to the CBD costs approximately $45–$60, depending on traffic conditions and your exact destination. Note that your driver will add the $4 airport fee that applies to all pick-ups at Perth Airport to your fare. Taxi ranks are available at the front of all terminals – look for signs directing you to the nearest one. There's also a taxi rank on Valentine Road in the General Aviation area.\n\n\n- **Rideshares:** If you prefer a rideshare, options like Ubre or DiDi are available at Perth Airport. Every terminal has a designated rideshare pick-up area. The price varies between $30 and $60 depending on your destination, the time of the day and how busy it is when you order. As with taxi rides, a $4 airport fee will be applied to your fare.\n\n\n- **Airport shuttles:** A few private companies offer shuttle bus services from Perth Airport. [The Perth Airport Shuttle](https://www.perthairportshuttle.com.au/) runs between the airport and city hotels and costs from $50 per person. This door-to-door service runs 24/7 and if you travel with a young family, you also have the option to request a baby seat. You can either meet your chauffeur at a designated pick-up point or request the meet-and-greet service where your chauffeur will hold a sign with your name. Alternatively, we’d recommend that you contact your hotel to see whether they have a shuttle bus service as it could be cheaper.\n\n\n- **Public transport:** Perth Airport is well connected to the city, making it easy to reach your destination using public transport. From the T1 and T2 terminals, you can catch a train from Airport Central Station to Perth Station in the CBD. The trip takes 18 minutes and costs $5. If you prefer to catch the bus, Bus Route 37 operates between Airport Central and Oats Street Station Monday to Friday.\n\n\n- **Car rentals:** Renting a car allows for more flexibility for your time in Perth. You can either hire a car before your trip to earn Velocity Points, or head to one of the major car rental agencies including Avis and Hertz, located at Perth Airport.\n\n\n## How to get around Perth\n\nWhile Perth’s city centre is compact and easy to explore on foot, getting around Perth to experience all it has to offer requires a bit more planning. Whether you’re looking to visit local attractions, enjoy nearby natural wonders, or relax at one of [the best beaches in Perth](https://www.virginaustralia.com/au/en/destinations/perth/10-best-beaches-perth/#:~:text=Esperance's%20Lucky%20Bay%20and%20Broome's,on%20Tripadvisor's%20list%20in%202022.), here's a breakdown of the best ways to explore the city and its surroundings.\n\n### Public transport\n\nPerth’s transport network, managed by Transperth, includes buses, trains, ferries, and free transit zones to simplify travel. In Perth, you can travel anywhere on the Transperth network, up to nine zones, for no more than a two-zone fare!\n\n#### SmartRider Card\n\nThe SmartRider card is a reusable smart card that allows you to save 10% compared to cash tickets. These cards are available for purchase at Transperth InfoCentres, such as those at Perth Station and Elizabeth Quay Bus Station, train stations, kiosks, and newsagents. The card allows you to tap on and off as you travel. SmartRider fares vary depending on zones, but a Standard SmartRider costs $20 and includes $10 of travel. If you are travelling using a SmartRider, your fare will automatically be capped at two zones. Another benefit to having the card is that every Sunday, travel is free for all SmartRider holders.\n\n#### Buses\n\nPerth Central Area Transit (CAT) buses are fast, convenient and, more importantly, free within the City of Perth. These free buses operate on four colour-coded routes (Red, Blue, Green, and Yellow) with stops near key attractions like Elizabeth Quay, Kings Park, and Northbridge​.     ​ Note that bus fares apply outside the City of Perth area, and you can only pay for your trip while you’re on the bus using your SmartRider card or cash.\n\nHere is a breakdown of the different CAT routes based on their colour:\n\n![Map of Perth’s free bus routes ](https://www.virginaustralia.com/content/dam/vaa/img/destinations/perth/getting-around/perth-free-bus-routes.jpg/_jcr_content/renditions/vaacore.web.720.0.jpg)\n\nCredit: Hello Perth\n\n**Blue CAT**\n\nBlue CAT buses travel North/South between Perth Busport and Kings Park via Perth Station and Elizabeth Quay Bus Station. They operate every day of the year, excluding Good Friday, Anzac Day and Christmas Day.\n\n**Green CAT**\n\nThese buses travel between Elizabeth Quay Bus Station and Leederville Station via City West Station. Trains on the green line operate Monday to Friday, excluding Good Friday, Anzac Day and Christmas Day.\n\n**Red CAT**\n\nBuses on the Red Cat travel East / West between Matagarup Bridge (East Perth) and West Perth, via Perth Station and Perth Underground Station. They operate every day of the year, excluding Good Friday, Anzac Day and Christmas Day.\n\n**Yellow CAT**\n\nThese buses travel between Claisebrook Station (East Perth) and West Perth, via Perth Station. Yellow CAT buses operate every day of the year, excluding Good Friday, Anzac Day and Christmas Day.\n\n#### Trains\n\n![Transperth Train in Perth Western Australia  ](https://www.virginaustralia.com/content/dam/vaa/img/destinations/perth/getting-around/perth-train.jpg/_jcr_content/renditions/vaacore.web.720.0.jpg)\n\nCredit: Rafael Ben-Ari from stock.adobe.com\n\nPerth's train network is a vital part of the city's public transportation system, providing an efficient and reliable way to travel around the metropolitan area. The network is operated by Transperth, and spans several lines connecting Perth's central business district (CBD) with the surrounding suburbs and regional areas. On average, the train journey from Perth Station to major locations within the city typically takes around 15-20 minutes.\n\nAnd the good news for people exploring the city is that Perth’s transport system has been designed with travellers in mind. Trains are integrated with the bus and ferry systems, all accessible via the SmartRider card. This card makes the entire travel experience more seamless, allowing passengers to tap on and off at any station.\n\nThere’s also a Free Transit Zone for trains that covers the area between City West, Elizabeth Quay and Claisebrook stations, making getting around Perth a breeze. To enjoy the free fares, tap on and off with a SmartRider card.\n\n![Map of Perth train lines and routes.  ](https://www.virginaustralia.com/content/dam/vaa/img/destinations/perth/getting-around/perth-train-routes.jpg/_jcr_content/renditions/vaacore.web.720.0.jpg)\n\nCredit: TransPerth\n\n**Key train lines in Perth include:**\n\n**Armadale Line:** The Armadale Line runs from the Perth CBD to Armadale in the southeast. It serves suburbs like Victoria Park, Cannington, and Gosnells, providing access to areas further south such as Kelmscott and Armadale. This line connects commuters from the southern suburbs to the city centre.\n\nThe Armadale Line is also useful for those heading to Optus Stadium for major events or concerts, as it's just a short hop from the station.\n\n**Yanchep Line (formerly known as Joondalup):**\n\nRunning from the city to Joondalup in the north, this line passes through suburban areas like Leederville, Subiaco, and Warwick. It serves the northern suburbs and offers a direct route to the expanding areas near Joondalup, a major retail hub.\n\nIf you're planning to visit Lake Monger, the Yanchep Line is the most convenient option.\n\n**Mandurah Line:** The Mandurah Line starts from the Perth CBD and heads south towards Mandurah. It passes through suburbs like Rockingham and Kwinana, making it a key transport option for those who are staying in the southern coastal areas. A great day trip destination from Mandurah is Penguin Island, and the Mandurah Line is perfect for those heading to the ferry terminal (you’ll have to disembark at Rockingham Station).\n\n**Fremantle Line:** Connecting the Perth CBD with Fremantle, this line serves the southwestern part of the metropolitan area. It passes through suburbs such as North Fremantle, Claremont, and Subiaco, making it ideal for those travelling to Cottesloe beach, Rottnest Island, Perth's port city and its surrounding areas.\n\nA day trip to Fremantle is one of the [best things to do in Perth](https://www.virginaustralia.com/au/en/destinations/perth/things-to-do-in-perth/) thanks to its vibrant arts scene and historical significance. Once there, stroll along Fremantle's waterfront, explore the Fremantle Markets, or visit the Western Australian Maritime Museum.\n\n**Thornlie Line:** The Thornlie Line branches off the Armadale Line and heads southeast, serving areas like Maddington and Gosnells. Note that the Armadale/Thornlie Line is shut between Victoria Park and Armadale/Thornlie stations until mid-2025..\n\nThe Thornlie Line is convenient for those looking to explore Westfield Carousel.\n\nReady to go? Book a flight to Perth now\n\n[Flights to Perth](https://www.virginaustralia.com/en-au/flights-to-perth)\n\n#### Ferries\n\n![A passenger ferry boat arrives from a Swan River cruise to dock in the Perth harbour of Queen Elizabeth Quay, Western Australia  ](https://www.virginaustralia.com/content/dam/vaa/img/destinations/perth/getting-around/perth-ferry-on-swan-river.jpg/_jcr_content/renditions/vaacore.web.720.0.jpg)\n\nCredit: David EP Dennis from stock.adobe.com\n\nFor a scenic journey, hop on a Transperth ferry at Elizabeth Quay using your SmartRider card. Ferries operate across the Swan River to South Perth, where you can visit the Perth Zoo or enjoy riverside dining. The short ride offers stunning views of the city skyline​​.\n\n![Map of Perth ferry route ](https://www.virginaustralia.com/content/dam/vaa/img/destinations/perth/getting-around/perth-ferry-route-map.jpg/_jcr_content/renditions/vaacore.web.720.0.jpg)\n\nCredit: Transperth\n\n#### Useful Perth public transport apps and resources\n\nWant to make your trip to Perth even easier? Check out these top apps that’ll guide you every step of the way!\n\n- **Transperth App:** One of the most popular Perth public transport apps is Transperth. The [Transperth App](https://www.transperth.wa.gov.au/app#:~:text=The%20free%20Transperth%20app%20is,on%20iOS%20and%20Android%20devices.) is a must-have for anyone using the city's public transport network. It offers real-time updates on buses, trains, and ferries, as well as a journey planner to help you map your route. You can check live schedules, track your next bus or train, and get alerts about any disruptions on your route. This app ensures you're always on time for your next destination. The Transperth app also allows you to manage your SmartRider card on the go. You can check your balance, top up your card, and view your transaction history directly from your phone, making it easier to stay on top of your travel needs.\n\n- **Transperth Journey Planner:** If you don’t want to download the app, the [Transperth Journey Planner](https://www.transperth.wa.gov.au/Journey-Planner) on their website helps you plan your trip ahead of time, giving you all the necessary details on routes, schedules, and fare pricing.\n\n\n- **Transit app:** Lastly, the [Transit app](https://transitapp.com/en/region/perth/transperth/ferry-ferry#download) is specifically designed for ferry trips, displaying ferry times and live updates.\n\n\n### Taxis and ridesharing\n\nWhether you're heading to your accommodation in Perth, the city’s attractions, the airport, or just need a quick ride across town, taxis and rideshare services are readily available in Perth.\n\nPerth’s taxis are metered, and you can either flag one down or book through a taxi app. Well-known companies like [13Cabs](https://www.13cabs.com.au/locations/perth/) and [Universal Cabs](https://universalcabs.com.au/) operate across the city, and their websites allow for quick bookings. [13Cabs](https://www.13cabs.com.au/locations/perth/) even has an app and provides an estimate of your fare. You’ll also find designated taxi ranks at popular locations such as shopping centres, Perth Airport, and major transport hubs.\n\nIf you prefer ridesharing, both Uber and DiDi are a convenient way of getting around Perth, offering a seamless and flexible transport option. Shebah is another rideshare service available in Perth. This all-female fleet is a great choice for women and children, ensuring a safe and comfortable journey.\n\n### Car rental and driving in Perth\n\nIf flexibility is a priority, consider hiring a car for your trip to Perth. Perth’s roads are easy to navigate, and having a car will make it easy to explore the many scenic drives from Perth, from the stunning coastal areas to the nearby [Swan Valley wineries](https://www.virginaustralia.com/au/en/destinations/perth/swan-valley-wineries/).\n\nFor those renting a car to self-drive, be mindful of:\n\n- **Parking:** Free parking is only available at select car parks in the CBD. For instance, the Cultural Centre offers three-hour free parking on weekends and the Pier Street Car Park is free at night between 6pm and 5:59am. Paid parking can be expensive during weekdays with an average of $6.70 for kerbside parking.\n\n\n- **Road rules:** Watch for speed limits and be aware of road signs, especially near pedestrian-heavy areas.\n\n\n### Cycling and scooters\n\n![A man cycling on an elizabeth bridge in Perth city](https://www.virginaustralia.com/content/dam/vaa/img/destinations/perth/getting-around/cycling-in-perth.jpg/_jcr_content/renditions/vaacore.web.720.0.jpg)\n\nCredit: anekoho from stock.adobe.com\n\nPerth is a bike-friendly city, featuring an extensive network of cycle paths and dual-use lanes that make it easy to explore on two wheels. There are plenty of bike hire options across the city, including the convenient hire stations at Point Fraser and Kings Park. For a fun and scenic ride, the Swan River Loop is a must! There, you’ll be able to cycle along the river’s stunning bridges and waterfront, passing scenic spots like Optus Stadium, Claisebrook Cove, and Elizabeth Quay.\n\nIf you’re in need of secure bike parking or charging, head over to the Smart Bike Hub at RAC Arena, which offers free bays for electric bikes and scooters. The city also provides detailed [cycling maps](https://www.transport.wa.gov.au/mediafiles/active-transport/at_cyc_map_perthfremantle-stirling.pdf). Whether you're looking for a leisurely ride or a way to get around the city, cycling in Perth is a convenient and scenic option.\n\n### Walking in Perth\n\nPerth is one of the world’s most walkable cities, with many of the city's key attractions, dining spots, [bars](https://www.virginaustralia.com/au/en/destinations/perth/best-bars-perth-eat-drink-and-be-merry/) and shopping districts located within easy walking distance of one another. The layout of the CBD is designed to be pedestrian-friendly, offering wide sidewalks, pedestrian-only zones, and vibrant streetscapes that make it easy to explore on foot - particularly if [Perth's weather](https://www.virginaustralia.com/au/en/destinations/perth/perth-climate/) is showing off! From the stunning Elizabeth Quay to the lively laneways in Northbridge, walking around Perth is an immersive experience.\n\n### Accessible transport options\n\nPerth offers a range of accessible transport options for travellers with mobility aids, such as wheelchairs, walkers, and prams.\n\nMost of the city’s buses, trains, and ferries are designed to be accessible, with ramps and spaces for passengers who need extra assistance.\n\nAdditionally, Perth’s trains, including those operating on the Armadale, Fremantle, and Joondalup lines, are all wheelchair-friendly, ensuring a smooth journey across the city. However not all train stations provide suitable access to people with reduced mobility. Transperth Map allows travellers to identify stations listed as “ [Independent Access”](https://www.transperth.wa.gov.au/Using-Transperth/Station-Facilities/Stations-Maps). These stations allow the majority of passengers to access the station independently either via ramps, pathways or lifts.\n\nWhen it comes to taxis, wheelchair-accessible services are available through providers like 13Cabs, which can be booked in advance to ensure a comfortable ride. Perth Airport is also fully equipped with accessible facilities, offering ramps, elevators, and mobility services to assist passengers with reduced mobility.\n\n## Perth transport FAQs\n\n- ##### What is the best way to get around Perth?\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\nThe best way to get around Perth depends on what you’re planning to do. If you’re sticking to the city centre and nearby attractions, the public transport system is a convenient and affordable option. Perth's train and bus network, operated by Transperth, covers a wide area, with the city’s Free Transit Zone making it easy to travel around the CBD at no cost. It’s also incredibly easy to get around the city by foot, so if you’re sticking to the city centre, there’s no need to have a car.\n\n\n\nFor those looking to explore beyond Perth’s centre, like the beautiful Swan River, Fremantle or Cottesloe Beach, trains or buses are quick and reliable. Alternatively, taxis and rideshare services like Uber are readily available for a more direct journey.\n\n- ##### Can you travel around Perth without a car?\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\nAbsolutely, it’s very easy to get around Perth without a car! Perth has an extensive and efficient public transport network that makes getting around the city and its suburbs easy without a car. Trains, buses, and ferries connect key locations with regular services throughout the day. The CBD is especially walkable, and many of the top [Perth attractions](https://www.virginaustralia.com/au/en/destinations/perth/things-to-do-in-perth/perth-attractions/) are within walking distance or accessible by public transport. Additionally, rideshare options like Uber and DiDi are always available. Many visitors find that they don’t need a car when staying in the city centre.\n\n- ##### Is Perth a walkable city?\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\n\nYes, Perth is a highly walkable city, especially in the central areas. With wide pedestrian-friendly streets, plenty of green spaces like Kings Park, and vibrant areas like Northbridge and Elizabeth Quay, walking is an excellent way to explore the city. Perth CBD is compact, meaning you can easily visit landmarks, shops, cafes, and more without needing to travel far. For longer distances, the public transport network will quickly get you to your destination.\n\n\n## Flights to Perth\n\nNow that you know Perth's public transport system like the back of your hand, it's time to book your flight and start exploring the city! Virgin Australia offers direct flights to Perth from major Australian cities, including:\n\n- [Sydney to Perth](https://www.virginaustralia.com/en-au/flights-from-sydney-to-perth?cmpid=ps:va:brand:gads:ao:na&gad_source=1&gclid=CjwKCAiA6t-6BhA3EiwAltRFGKOM-KjnGMLCDzFP9FTwufoLhKkK5hs5N32GfizSimTO8UZYG_R3OhoCcv8QAvD_BwE&gclsrc=aw.ds)\n- [Brisbane to Perth](https://www.virginaustralia.com/en-au/flights-from-brisbane-to-perth?cmpid=ps:va:brand:gads:ao:na&gad_source=1&gclid=CjwKCAiA6t-6BhA3EiwAltRFGIRPg2CXuAVG8mOzUpKPSK7NHULDX9WAfXHDa8-ZJu0UT3yebp3UDhoCxRAQAvD_BwE&gclsrc=aw.ds)\n- [Adelaide to Perth](https://www.virginaustralia.com/en-au/flights-from-adelaide-to-perth?cmpid=ps:va:brand:gads:ao:na&gad_source=1&gclid=CjwKCAiA6t-6BhA3EiwAltRFGBAvPCQppeVtdyzT_xeoidvgaQwV8f9CqJl1jlJPo7n_xZV3OVrrvxoCUWsQAvD_BwE&gclsrc=aw.ds)\n- [Melbourne to Perth](https://www.virginaustralia.com/en-au/flights-from-melbourne-to-perth?cmpid=ps:va:brand:gads:ao:na&gad_source=1&gclid=CjwKCAiA6t-6BhA3EiwAltRFGJLzjYAwp0iFd9S4ATjoOU-YXoRNqf2Tq34DVblkiYsFD5qZ3ABoMhoCfywQAvD_BwE&gclsrc=aw.ds)\n- [Canberra to Perth](https://www.virginaustralia.com/en-au/flights-from-canberra-to-perth?cmpid=ps:va:brand:gads:ao:na&gad_source=1&gclid=CjwKCAiA6t-6BhA3EiwAltRFGMIiBiWdCnK267tIJ5vnwHnNIEshffCntWZ6HmgxucQmR7QBPH_5bhoCDP8QAvD_BwE&gclsrc=aw.ds)\n- [Gold Coast to Perth](https://www.virginaustralia.com/en-au/flights-from-gold-coast-to-perth?cmpid=ps:va:brand:gads:ao:na&gad_source=1&gclid=CjwKCAiA6t-6BhA3EiwAltRFGF_v2SvVs-yXNkd0knI_z7fXd6-WAJk7js-y15IrN9Kr-d2vNluoLRoC_CAQAvD_BwE&gclsrc=aw.ds)\n- [Hobart to Perth](https://www.virginaustralia.com/en-au/flights-from-hobart-to-perth?cmpid=ps:va:brand:gads:ao:na&gad_source=1&gclid=CjwKCAiA6t-6BhA3EiwAltRFGC3nHDnbdwgv5a3MHjAgZT1EuKQtFDfP4_4kBg5lISX-jREnlWwvoxoCnxgQAvD_BwE&gclsrc=aw.ds)\n\nReady to book? We have everything for your trip\n\n[Flights](https://www.virginaustralia.com/en-au/flights-to-perth) [Hotel](https://www.virginaustralia.com/en-au/hotels-in-perth) [Car hire](https://carhire.virginaustralia.com/?icmpid=va:anc:va_dest:carhire:car_trawler_ao:0) [Holidays](https://www.virginaustralia.com/en-au/holidays-in-perth)\n\n## Related content\n\n![Where to stay in Perth – Area and accommodation guide](https://www.virginaustralia.com/content/dam/vaa/img/destinations/perth/where-to-stay/where-to-stay-in-perth-display.jpg)\n\nWhere to stay\n\n### Where to stay in Perth – Area and accommodation guide\n\nLooking for the best area to stay in Perth? Whether you’re a first-time visitor, in town for business, or want to live like a local, our area and hot recommendations have you covered.\n\n![20 Perth attractions to visit on your next holiday ](https://www.virginaustralia.com/content/dam/vaa/img/destinations/perth/attractions/perth-attractions-bell-tower-display.jpg)\n\nAttractions\n\n### 20 Perth attractions to visit on your next holiday\n\nFrom beautiful beaches and wildlife opportunities to historic sites and iconic landmarks these are the top attractions to visit in Perth for families, tourists and culture seekers.\n\n![30 top things to do in Perth – your ultimate travel guide](https://www.virginaustralia.com/content/dam/vaa/img/destinations/perth/things-to-do/things-to-do-in-perth-display.jpg)\n\nAttractions\n\n### 30 top things to do in Perth – your ultimate travel guide\n\nFrom pristine beaches and sprawling parks to impressive outdoor attractions and landmarks, these are the top things to see and do in Perth.\n\n![Website feedback](https://syd1.qualtrics.com/WRQualtricsSiteIntercept/Graphic.php?IM=IM_afbKg7FUGKt1PxQ)\n\n![Close](https://siteintercept.qualtrics.com/WRQualtricsShared/Graphics/siteintercept/wr-dialog-close-btn-black.png)",

        "url": "https://translink.com.au/tickets-and-fares/50-cent-fares",
        "title": "50 cent fares - Translink",
        "description": "50 cent fares | Translink\n\n![](https://translink.com.au/sites/default/files/styles/16_9_aspect_ratio_large/public/acquiadam-assets/6352_50-cents-vector_Homepage_Web-tile_D01.png?itok=yb6nrMff)\n\nPublic transport fares are a 50 cent flat rate, across all zones and modes on the Translink network across Queensland.\n\nThis includes all regional urban buses, and bus, train (excluding Airtrain), ferry, tram and on demand services in South East Queensland.\n\n### Services\n- Queensland Rail City Train services (excluding Airtrain services)\n- buses (excluding dedicated school buses)\n- Brisbane City ferries and Southern Moreton Bay Island Ferry\n- trams\n\n### Airtrain\nThe 50 cent flat rate does not apply to Airtrain services. You can use your _go_ card or buy your ticket online from [Airtrain](https://www.airtrain.com.au/). Find out more about [Airtrain fares](https://www.airtrain.com.au/) or phone (07) 3216 3308 or 1800 119 091.\n\n### Ticketing options\nThe 50 cent flat applies to all _go_ card, credit/debit card and paper ticket payments.\n\nThe SEQ network is cashless, but you can use cash to top up your _go_ card at any of our fare machines at bus and train stations, or at our [_go_ card retailers](https://translink.com.au/tickets-and-fares/go-card/locations).\n\n### Concessions and discounts\n#### Concessions\nThe 50% concession fares does not apply during this time. Fares will be a flat 50 cent rate for everyone using public transport.\n\n#### Seniors\nIf you're a [Senior Concession Card holder](https://translink.com.au/tickets-and-fares/concessions/seniors), then you can continue to travel free on Brisbane City Council buses during off-peak travel times.\n\n### [Regional Queensland](https://translink.com.au/tickets-and-fares/50-cent-fares#1389446)\nIf you're travelling by urban bus in regional Queensland then you can pay by cash onboard or via any of the existing ticketing options.\n\n### Services\nThe 50 cent flat fare applies to all Translink network regional bus services in the following areas:\n\n- Bowen\n- Bundaberg\n- Cairns\n- Gladstone\n- Gympie\n- Innisfail\n- Kilcoy\n- Mackay\n- Rockhampton and Yeppoon\n- Sunshine Coast Hinterland\n- Toowoomba (including [on demand](https://translink.com.au/travel-with-us/on-demand/toowoomba))\n- Townsville\n- Warwick\n- Whitsundays\n\n### Ticketing options\n#### Concessions\nThe 50% concession fares will not apply during this time. Fares will be a flat 50 cent rate for everyone using public transport.\n\n[**How to get a go card**\\\n\\\n![](https://translink.com.au/sites/default/files/styles/1_1_aspect_ratio_small/public/acquiadam-assets/go-card-fan_1280x%201025.png?itok=uIBX8o5y)](https://translink.com.au/tickets-and-fares/go-card) [**Travel tips**\\\n\\\n![](https://translink.com.au/sites/default/files/styles/1_1_aspect_ratio_small/public/acquiadam-assets/George%20Hailing%20bus2.jpg?itok=DuQ8MO3s)](https://translink.com.au/travel-with-us/using-public-transport) [**Park 'n' ride**\\\n\\\n![](https://translink.com.au/sites/default/files/styles/1_1_aspect_ratio_small/public/acquiadam-assets/Grovely%20016A5.jpg?itok=xHx8Ho0z)](https://translink.com.au/travel-with-us/parking) [**Active travel**\\\n\\\n![](https://translink.com.au/sites/default/files/styles/1_1_aspect_ratio_small/public/acquiadam-assets/Translink_B4A4105.jpg?itok=frmK5e6L)](https://translink.com.au/travel-with-us/cycling-and-walking)",
        "position": 3,
        "markdown": "50 cent fares \\| Translink\n\n![](https://translink.com.au/sites/default/files/styles/16_9_aspect_ratio_large/public/acquiadam-assets/6352_50-cents-vector_Homepage_Web-tile_D01.png?itok=yb6nrMff)\n\nPublic transport fares are a 50 cent flat rate, across all zones and modes on the Translink network across Queensland.\n\nThis includes all regional urban buses, and bus, train (excluding Airtrain), ferry, tram and on demand services in South East Queensland.\n\n### [South East Queensland](https://translink.com.au/tickets-and-fares/50-cent-fares\\#1389436)\n\n### Services\n\nThe 50 cent flat fare applies to all:\n\n- Queensland Rail City Train services (excluding Airtrain services)\n- buses (excluding dedicated school buses)\n- Brisbane City ferries and Southern Moreton Bay Island Ferry\n- trams\n- [on demand services](https://translink.com.au/travel-with-us/on-demand).\n\n### Airtrain\n\nThe 50 cent flat rate does not apply to Airtrain services. You can use your _go_ card or buy your ticket online from [Airtrain](https://www.airtrain.com.au/). Find out more about [Airtrain fares](https://www.airtrain.com.au/) or phone (07) 3216 3308 or 1800 119 091.\n\n### Ticketing options\n\nThe 50 cent flat applies to all _go_ card, credit/debit card and paper ticket payments.\n\nThe SEQ network is cashless, but you can use cash to top up your _go_ card at any of our fare machines at bus and train stations, or at our [_go_ card retailers](https://translink.com.au/tickets-and-fares/go-card/locations).\n\n### Concessions and discounts\n\n#### Off-peak travel discount\n\nThe 20% discount on off-peak travel does not apply during this time. All travel across zones and times of day will be a flat rate of 50 cents.\n\n#### Concessions\n\nThe 50% concession fares does not apply during this time. Fares will be a flat 50 cent rate for everyone using public transport.\n\n#### Seniors\n\nIf you're a [Senior Concession Card holder](https://translink.com.au/tickets-and-fares/concessions/seniors), then you can continue to travel free on Brisbane City Council buses during off-peak travel times.\n\n#### Children\n\n- Children aged five to 14 can still travel free on weekends using their orange child _go_ card.\n- Children aged four years old and under can still travel for free at all times.\n\n#### Frequent journey discounts\n\nThe current frequency based discounts such as '8 journeys and half price' and 'One, Two, Free' doesn't apply during this time.\n\n### [Regional Queensland](https://translink.com.au/tickets-and-fares/50-cent-fares\\#1389446)\n\nIf you're travelling by urban bus in regional Queensland then you can pay by cash onboard or via any of the existing ticketing options.\n\n### Services\n\nThe 50 cent flat fare applies to all Translink network regional bus services in the following areas:\n\n- Bowen\n- Bundaberg\n- Cairns\n- Fraser Coast (including [on demand](https://translink.com.au/travel-with-us/on-demand/hervey-bay))\n- Gladstone\n- Gympie\n- Innisfail\n- Kilcoy\n- Mackay\n- Rockhampton and Yeppoon\n- Sunshine Coast Hinterland\n- Toowoomba (including [on demand](https://translink.com.au/travel-with-us/on-demand/toowoomba))\n- Townsville\n- Warwick\n- Whitsundays\n\n### Ticketing options\n\nYou will be able to pay in cash onboard the bus as you do now.\n\n#### Concessions\n\nThe 50% concession fares will not apply during this time. Fares will be a flat 50 cent rate for everyone using public transport.\n\n[**How to get a go card**\\\\\n\\\\\n![](https://translink.com.au/sites/default/files/styles/1_1_aspect_ratio_small/public/acquiadam-assets/go-card-fan_1280x%201025.png?itok=uIBX8o5y)](https://translink.com.au/tickets-and-fares/go-card) [**Travel tips**\\\\\n\\\\\n![](https://translink.com.au/sites/default/files/styles/1_1_aspect_ratio_small/public/acquiadam-assets/George%20Hailing%20bus2.jpg?itok=DuQ8MO3s)](https://translink.com.au/travel-with-us/using-public-transport) [**Park 'n' ride**\\\\\n\\\\\n![](https://translink.com.au/sites/default/files/styles/1_1_aspect_ratio_small/public/acquiadam-assets/Grovely%20016A5.jpg?itok=xHx8Ho0z)](https://translink.com.au/travel-with-us/parking) [**Active travel**\\\\\n\\\\\n![](https://translink.com.au/sites/default/files/styles/1_1_aspect_ratio_small/public/acquiadam-assets/Translink_B4A4105.jpg?itok=frmK5e6L)](https://translink.com.au/travel-with-us/cycling-and-walking)",

        "url": "https://sg.trip.com/guide/transport/perth-airport-to-city.html",
        "title": "Perth Airport to City : Transport Options, Train, Taxi, Shuttle ...",
        "description": "17 Dec 2025 · The train fare from Perth Airport (Airport Central Station) to the city is capped at the 2-zone rate, which is about AUD $5.00.",

        "markdown": "[Trip.com](https://sg.trip.com/?locale=en-SG&curr=SGD)\n\nApp\n\nSGD\n\nCustomer support\n\n[Find bookings](https://sg.trip.com/passport/ordersearch/?locale=en-SG&curr=SGD)\n\nSign in/register\n\n# Perth Airport to City : Transport Options, Train, Taxi, Shuttle & Rideshare\n\n![Author's Profile Picture](https://dimg04.c-ctrip.com/images/1hq3512000qjo25z69E46.jpg?proc=format/f_webp)\n\nBy Feyna\n\nDecember 17, 2025\n\n![Perth Airport to City](https://dimg04.tripcdn.com/images/0M72b12000pw9y80mA950_Q20.jpg)\n\nTravelling to [Western Australia](https://sg.trip.com/guide/transport/melbourne-airport-to-city.html) soon? One of the most common questions for visitors is **how to get from Perth Airport to the city** quickly and affordably.\n\nWhether you prefer the train, taxi, shuttle, rideshare, or private transfer, Perth offers several convenient options for every type of traveller.\n\nThis guide covers everything you need to know about transport from Perth Airport to city, including travel times, fares, and the best option for your budget.\n\nFlights\n\nNew Users Save Up to S$20 on First Flight Booking\n\nTRIPFLIGHT26SG\n\nHotels\n\n10% Off for First Booking\n\nGet 10% off new user hotel coupon on your first booking (with cap).\n\nClaim\n\nHotels\n\nHomes & Apts Deal\n\nUp to S$78 OFF Homes & Apts\n\nClaim\n\nCar Rentals\n\nCar Rental Promo Code - 8% Off\n\nTRIPCAR8\n\n## Quick Guide: Perth Airport to City\n\n![Perth International Airport](https://dimg04.tripcdn.com/images/0M76712000pwa7tneAA24_Q50.jpg?proc=format/f_webp)\n\nPerth Airport is located approximately **12 km east of the Perth CBD**. It has two terminals:\n\n- **Terminal 1 (T1)**: International flights\n- **Terminal 2 (T2)**: Domestic flights\n\n| **Transport Option** | **Time** | **Cost (AUD)** | **Pros** | **Cons** |\n| Train (T1) | 15–20 min | $4.80–$5.50 | Fast, cheap, reliable | Luggage during rush hour |\n| Bus | 30–50 min | $4–$5 | Affordable | Slower, may require transfer |\n| Taxi | 20–30 min | $45–$60 | Direct, 24/7 | Expensive |\n| Rideshare | 20–30 min | $35–$50 | Convenient, app-based | Surge pricing |\n| Private Transfer | 20–30 min | Varies | Comfortable, door-to-door | More expensive |\n| Car Rental | 20–30 min | $50+ per day | Flexible travel | Parking, traffic |\n\n[Australia 5G eSIM from S$0.35](https://sg.trip.com/things-to-do/detail/53821812/)\n\n## Train From Perth Airport to City\n\n![Train Perth Airport to City](https://dimg04.tripcdn.com/images/0M71312000pwaboydC255_Q50.jpg?proc=format/f_webp)\n\nThe Airport Line train is the fastest and most affordable way to reach the Perth CBD. It is the best for solo travellers, budget travellers and light luggage.\n\n### 📍Train route and Key Stops\n\n- Trains operate from Airport Central Station (T1)\n- Terminal 2 passengers can take a free shuttle to T1\n- The train connects directly to Perth Station in the CBD\n\nPerth Airport (T1 & T2) ➝ Airport Central ➝ Redcliffe ➝ Bayswater ➝ Meltham ➝ Mount Lawley ➝ East Perth ➝ Perth Station\n\n![Line Map Perth Airport to City](https://dimg04.tripcdn.com/images/0M72x12000pwahunnB1B3_Q60.jpg?proc=format/f_webp)\n\n| **Station** | **What It Connects To** | **Travel Time from Airport** | **Notes** |\n| **Airport Central (T1/T2)** | Main airport terminal link | — | Direct walkway + lifts/escalators to terminals |\n| **Redcliffe** | Local bus routes & hotels | 3–4 mins | Good for nearby accommodation |\n| **Bayswater** | Joondalup & Midland Line interchange | 7–8 mins | Major transfer station |\n| **Meltham** | Residential area | 10–11 mins | Quiet stop, fewer services |\n| **Mount Lawley** | Café/dining precinct | 12–13 mins | Popular local suburb |\n| **East Perth** | Bus terminal & interstate rail | 14–15 mins | For regional connections |\n| **Perth Station (City Centre)** | CBD, shopping, hotels | 18 min | Final stop — closest to most city hotels |\n\n### 💸 **Ticket Price and Where to Buy**\n\n- The train fare from Perth Airport (Airport Central Station) to the city is capped at the 2-zone rate, which is about AUD $5.00.\n- If you use a SmartRider card, you get a discount, typically 10% off the cash fare.\n- You can also use contactless payment (credit/debit card or mobile pay) on the station card readers, this usually gives you the same discounted fare as SmartRider.\n\n#### Where to Buy Tickets:\n\n| **Option** | **Details** |\n| **Ticket Machines** | Ticket vending machines available at **Airport Central Station** where you can buy cash tickets. |\n| **SmartRider Cards** | You can buy a SmartRider card at the airport (e.g., baggage services, info desks) or at Transperth retail outlets. |\n| **SmartRider Top-Up** | After purchase, you can top up your SmartRider at stations or through vending machines. |\n| **On-Board Purchase** | Buying a ticket onboard is not recommended — best to buy before travel. |\n\n**💖Note:** The train frequencyevery 10–15 mins during peak hours\n\nFlights\n\nNew Users Save Up to S$20 on First Flight Booking\n\nTRIPFLIGHT26SG\n\nHotels\n\n10% Off for First Booking\n\nGet 10% off new user hotel coupon on your first booking (with cap).\n\nClaim\n\nHotels\n\nHomes & Apts Deal\n\nUp to S$78 OFF Homes & Apts\n\nClaim\n\nCar Rentals\n\nCar Rental Promo Code - 8% Off\n\nTRIPCAR8\n\n## Airport Bus Services\n\n![Bus Perth Airport to City](https://dimg04.tripcdn.com/images/0M71412000pwbhodt3FCF_Q50.jpg?proc=format/f_webp)\n\nTransperth also runs bus services connecting the airport to city. It is best for budget travellers who don't mind slower routes.\n\n### 📍Bus route and Key Stops\n\n| **Bus Route** | **Description** |\n| **Bus 292** | Connects **Perth Airport** to **Elizabeth Quay** (City). |\n| **Bus 37** | Serves major suburbs along the route and connects the airport to key city areas. |\n\n### 💸 **Ticket Price and Where to Buy**\n\n| **Payment Method** | **Fare / Price** | **Where to Buy / Pay** |\n| **Cash Ticket** | ~ **AUD $4.70** for a 2-zone trip (Transperth standard fare) | Pay **on board** the bus. Transperth notes that no change is given, so you need to tender the exact fare. |\n| **SmartRider Card** | Provides a **10% discount** off cash fare. | Buy at **Smarte Carte** on the ground floor of Terminal 1, or from Transperth retail outlets. |\n\n💡 **Tips:** When paying with cash, make sure you have the **exact fare**, as Transperth drivers generally _do not give change_\n\n## Taxi Services\n\n![Taxi Perth Airport to City](https://dimg04.tripcdn.com/images/0M72x12000pwbp5dfF8CD_Q50.jpg?proc=format/f_webp)\n\nTaxis are available **24/7** at the designated taxi ranks outside both terminals. It is best for first-time visitors, business travellers and group travellers.\n\n### 📍 Where to Get a Taxi\n\n- The taxi ranks are located right outside the front of all terminals, just follow the signs when you exit arrivals.\n- The main taxi companies serving the airport are **Black & White Cabs** and **Swan Taxis**.\n\n**🔥Important:** There is a **$4.50 airport pickup fee** added to taxi fares when leaving from the terminals.\n\n### 💰Perth Airport to City Taxi Fare\n\n- **Taxi Fare:** Usually **AUD $45–$60**, depending on traffic\n- **Payment:** Cards, cash, EFTPOS accepted\n\n### ⏱️ Travel Time\n\n**Journey Time**: 20–30 minutes (depending on traffic)\n\nBest Hotels in Perth\n\nSee All Now](https://sg.trip.com/hotels/east-perth-hotel-detail-136100751/the-green-nest-cozy-stay-near-cbd-free-parking/)\n\n## Rideshare Options (Uber, DiDi, Ola)\n\n![Rideshare Perth Airport to City](https://dimg04.tripcdn.com/images/0M70i12000pwdmr0kE342_Q50.jpg?proc=format/f_webp)\n\nRideshare apps are widely available in Perth. Pick-up points are clearly marked at both terminals. It is best for budget-conscious travellers who still want a direct ride.\n\n### 🚖 Taxi vs Grab\n\n| **Factor** | **Taxi** | **Grab / Rideshare (Uber, DiDi)** |\n| **Cost Estimate** | ~ **A$45–$60** for a regular taxi ride from the airport to the city. Some maxi-taxis are more expensive (~A$65–85). | UberX or DiDi from the airport typically costs **A$38–$55** depending on time, demand, and traffic. There’s also a **A$4.50 airport pickup fee** for rideshares. |\n| **Availability** | Taxis are available 24/7 from the taxi ranks outside each terminal. | There are dedicated rideshare pickup zones at the airport for Uber and DiDi. |\n| **Convenience** | Very straightforward, especially if you have a lot of luggage — meter-based fare means no surprises if traffic is predictable. | Convenient, app-based. You can choose different ride types (XL, comfort, etc.) depending on luggage or group size. |\n| **Price Variability** | Fare is metered, so it’s more predictable (plus airport levy). | Price can surge depending on demand, time of day, and ride type — so cost can vary more. |\n| **Best For** | Travellers who prefer a fixed, metered fare or who may not be familiar with ride apps. Good for groups or heavy luggage. | Travellers comfortable with apps, want transparency in pricing, or are looking for slightly cheaper / more flexible ride options. |\n\n💖 **Note:** Journey time for rideshare is 20–30 minutes\n\n## Private Airport Transfers & Shuttle Services\n\n![Airport Transfer Perth Airport to City](https://dimg04.tripcdn.com/images/0M74s12000pwcp0hy879B_Q50.jpg?proc=format/f_webp)\n\nA number of private shuttle companies operate airport-to-hotel transfers. It is for travellers who are looking for comfort and convenience and travellers who are staying in hotels without easy public transport access.\n\n- **Private Transfers**: Pre-booked cars or vans that meet you at arrivals\n- **Shared Shuttle Services**: Economical for solo travellers or groups\n\n### ⏱ Duration & Availability\n\n- Perth City Airport Shuttle runs 24/7, so you'll always have an option to catch a shuttle at any hour.\n- The shared shuttle from Perth Airport to the city typically takes about 30 minutes to 1 hour, depending on traffic and the number of drop-off stops\n- According to Perth City Airport Shuttle: their 24/7 service means they can pick you up from any terminal at nearly any time.\n- For terminal transfers (between T1/T2 and T3/T4), Perth Airport provides a **free shuttle**:\n\n| **Time** | **Frequency** |\n| 06:00 – 22:00 | Every 20 minutes |\n| 22:00 – 06:00 | Every 30 minutes |\n\n[Best Perth SIM card Packages](https://sg.trip.com/things-to-do/experiences/perth-simcard/)\n\n## Car Rental\n\n![Car Rental Perth Airport to City](https://dimg04.tripcdn.com/images/0M76i12000pwc7rvlEA5D_Q50.jpg?proc=format/f_webp)\n\nSeveral major car rental companies operate at Perth Airport, including Avis, Budget, Hertz, Europcar, and Thrifty.\n\n| **Car Rental Company** | **Operating Hours (Daily)** | **Notes / After-Hours** |\n| **Avis** | 06:30 AM – 11:30 PM | After-hours returns via key drop-box |\n| **Budget** | 06:30 AM – 11:30 PM | After-hours returns available |\n| **Hertz** | 07:00 AM – 10:59 PM | Check with counter for after-hours pickup |\n| **Enterprise Rent‑A‑Car** | 07:00 AM – 10:30 PM | After-hours returns possible depending on booking |\n\n### **💸 Cost Range**\n\n| **Car Category** | **Typical Daily Cost (AUD)** |\n| **Economy / Small** | **~ $34 – $65** |\n| **Intermediate / Compact** | **~ $50 – $80** |\n| **SUV** | **~ $60 – $140+** |\n| **Full-Size / 4WD** | **~ $90 – $250** |\n| **Premium / Luxury** | **~ $114 – $200+** |\n\n### Things That May Increase Cost:\n\n- Optional insurance / The train from Perth Airport to city is the fastest and most affordable method, while taxis and rideshares offer door-to-door convenience.\n\nTravellers who prefer comfort or are staying far from the CBD may find the Perth Airport to city shuttle or private transfers more suitable.\n\nWhether you're travelling for business or exploring [Western Australia](https://sg.trip.com/guide/transport/avalon-airport-to-city.html), this guide ensures you have smooth, stress-free transport from Perth Airport to city from the moment you land.\n\n## FAQ: Perth Airport to City\n\n- ### How do I get from Perth Airport to the city?\n\n\n\n\n\n\nYou can travel via train, bus, taxi, rideshare (Uber/DiDi), shuttle services, or car rental. The fastest and cheapest option is usually the Airport Line train.\n\n\n- ### Where do I catch a taxi or rideshare at Perth Airport?\n\n\n\n\n\n\nTaxi ranks and rideshare pickup points are outside arrivals at all terminals. Airport pickup fees may apply (~AUD $4.50).\n\n\n- ### Are there car rental services at Perth Airport?\n\n\n\n\n\n\nYes, major providers include Avis, Budget, Hertz, Enterprise, SIXT, and others, with counters inside the terminals.\n\n\n- ### What’s the best option for solo travellers?\n\n\n\n\n\n\nThe train from Perth Airport to city is fast, cheap, and convenient.\n\n\n\nDisclaimer: This article has been provided by an individual contributor or third party platform. community libraries have been installed at Lakelands, Ellenbrook, and Alkimos stations.         "title": "Welcome to Perth: Preparing For Your Trip - Pan Pacific Hotels Group",
        "description": "# Getting around Perth | Pan Pacific Perth | Preparing for your Trip\nUpcoming Hotels and Serviced Suites\n\n# Welcome To Perth: Preparing For Your Trip\n### How to Get Around Perth, Western Australia’s Largest City\nAre you planning a trip to Perth? The capital of Western Australia, Perth offers an exciting mix of cosmopolitan pleasures and verdant scenery.\n\nA gateway to the famous Margaret River region of lush vineyards, Perth is also near historic Fremantle and Rottnest island, known for rich wildlife and pristine beaches.\n\nBookmark [Pan Pacific Perth](https://www.panpacific.com/en/hotels-and-resorts/pp-perth.html)'s handy guide to getting around Perth and get the most out of your visit.\n\n### Travelling from Perth Airport to the City\n![ perth airport taxis](https://www.panpacific.com/content/dam/pphg-revamp/en/ppper/the-hotel/destination-insights/welcome-to-perth-preparing-for-your-trip/taxi-perth-airport.jpg)\n\n![ perth airport taxis](https://www.panpacific.com/content/dam/pphg-revamp/en/ppper/the-hotel/destination-insights/welcome-to-perth-preparing-for-your-trip/taxi-perth-airport.jpg)\n\n_Image: Shutterstock_\n\nPublic transportation out of [Perth airport](https://www.perthairport.com.au/) can be tricky because it has no rail line to the city. Depending on how much time you have to get downtown, choose from a private charter vehicle, taxi, or bus.\n\nPrivate charter vehicles may be ideal if you are on a tight schedule or with a group. Drivers will meet and escort you to your vehicle. Vehicle services include [Exceed Chauffeur Services](https://www.tripadvisor.com.sg/Attraction_Review-g255103-d7697553-Reviews-Exceed_Chauffeur_Services-Perth_Greater_Perth_Western_Australia.html) (+61 410 669 525) and [GranTourismo Range Rover](http://www.grantourismo.com.au/) (+61 419 998 887).\n\nPrefer to hail a cab? Taxi stands are located outside each terminal. Or dial +61 131 008 for Black and White Cabs that serve Perth, or +61 13 13 30 for Swan Taxis. Uber is also available in Perth.\n\nConsider [airport bus services to Elizabeth Quay Bus Station downtown](http://www.perthairport.com.au/to-and-from-the-airport/transport-options/public-transport) if you are not in a hurry. Transperth bus 380 travels from terminals 1 and 2, and bus 40 runs from terminals 3 and 4 to Elizabeth Quay Bus Station.\n\n### Getting Around Perth City: Bus, Car, Train, and Taxi Options\nPlease accept cookies to access this content.\n\nClean and safe, Perth’s public transit system is formed by an extensive network of buses, trains, and ferries.\n\nGovernment-operated under Transperth, Perth’s [railway system](http://www.transperth.wa.gov.au/timetables/results?train=) and buses allow you to travel with a reusable SmartRider card that cuts 15%-25% off regular fares. SmartRider cards are available at Transperth Information Centres and retail outlets across town.\n\nIf you are staying downtown, you can also hop on and off the free [Central Area Transit (CAT)](http://www.transperth.wa.gov.au/timetables/cat-timetables) buses that run daily in the city centre. CAT bus stops also have real-time route information that keeps you informed about bus arrival times.\n\nSimilarly, travel for free on any bus within the city’s free transit zone (FTZ). When travelling outside the Perth FTZ, however, do buy a ticket on board or pay with a SmartRider card.\n\nTaxi stands are also located conveniently around the city. If you prefer to travel on water, you could catch the scenic [Transperth ferry service](http://www.transperth.wa.gov.au/timetables/results?Ferry=) between Elizabeth Quay Jetty and Mends St Jetty near the Perth Zoo. This ferry route runs long hours daily.\n\n### Getting To Know Perth’s Weather\n![perth cycling in good weather](https://www.panpacific.com/content/dam/pphg-revamp/en/ppper/the-hotel/destination-insights/welcome-to-perth-preparing-for-your-trip/swan-river-perth.jpg)\n\n![perth cycling in good weather](https://www.panpacific.com/content/dam/pphg-revamp/en/ppper/the-hotel/destination-insights/welcome-to-perth-preparing-for-your-trip/swan-river-perth.jpg)\n\n_Image: Pixabay_\n\nUnlike cities in Australia’s southern tip, Perth weather is generally mild and comfortable. Dry and balmy summer falls between December to February as Perth is in the southern hemisphere. Temperatures range from 18°C to 30°C (64°F to 86°F) then, with breezy afternoons blowing the heat away.\n\nAutumn in Perth from March to May is marked by warm, sunny days and comfortably cool nights. Temperatures fall between 14°C to 26°C (57°F to 79°F) with evening temperatures rarely dropping below 10°C (50°F). Occasional showers and humidity can crop up in autumn, however, so bring your favourite umbrella.\n\nRain and thunderstorms are common in winter, from June to August. Temperatures may fluctuate more widely during this season, so do dress in layers including a waterproof outer layer if possible. It can get quite chilly at night with temperatures running from 8°C to 19°C (47°F to 66°F) in winter.\n\nSpring arrives in Perth from September to November with sunny days and little rain. Daily temperatures range from 12°C to 23°C (53°F to 73°F). Wildflowers blossom in Perth’s parks and gardens at this time, so if you suffer from hay fever, pop your medication in your luggage!\n\n### Where to Shop in Perth\n![perth city shopping street](https://www.panpacific.com/content/dam/pphg-revamp/en/ppper/the-hotel/destination-insights/welcome-to-perth-preparing-for-your-trip/perth-city-hay-street-mall.jpg)\n\n![perth city shopping street](https://www.panpacific.com/content/dam/pphg-revamp/en/ppper/the-hotel/destination-insights/welcome-to-perth-preparing-for-your-trip/perth-city-hay-street-mall.jpg)\n\nShopping for souvenirs in Perth? Check out the city’s Murray and Hay Street Malls, West End and Wesley Quarter, and Watertown Brand Outlet Centre.\n\nFor a trip out of the city, consider spending your Aussie dollars in suburban shopping locations such as Subiaco, Claremont, Cottesloe, and Fremantle. Scoot down from Perth via the Fremantle train that Experience Perth recommends. Peruse the Visit Perth City website for their comprehensive shop directory.\n\nYou may also wish to pick up some handmade gifts for your friends back home.\n\n### Where To Catch Up On Event Listings and News in Perth\n![community newspapers perth](https://www.panpacific.com/content/dam/pphg-revamp/en/ppper/the-hotel/destination-insights/welcome-to-perth-preparing-for-your-trip/newspaper.jpg)\n\n![community newspapers perth](https://www.panpacific.com/content/dam/pphg-revamp/en/ppper/the-hotel/destination-insights/welcome-to-perth-preparing-for-your-trip/newspaper.jpg)\n\n_Image: Pixabay_\n\n[PerthNow](http://www.perthnow.com.au/), [The West Australian](https://thewest.com.au/latest), and [WA Today](http://www.watoday.com.au/) are also indispensable go-to sources of Perth updates before your trip. Beyond these, you could also do a quick scroll through [Google News’ Perth page](https://www.google.com.sg/search?q=google+news+perth&rlz=1C1CHBF_enSG696SG696&oq=google+news+perth&aqs=chrome..69i57j69i64.1930j0j7&sourceid=chrome&ie=UTF-8#tbm=nws&q=perth) to get you up to speed on local events on the go.\n\nNo time to do your own research? Simply drop by Pan Pacific Perth’s concierge desk to seek the expert opinions of our [Les Clefs d’Or Concierges](http://blog.panpacific.com/hotels/meet-our-stellar-concierges-pan-pacific-perth/).\n\n**Opening hours:**\n\nPerth city shopping hours: Monday to Thursday: 9:00am to 9:00pm, Friday: 9:00am to 9:00pm, Saturday: 9:00am to 5:00pm, Sunday: noon to 6:00pm.\n\nFremantle shopping hours: Monday to Thursday: 8:00am to 7:00pm, Friday: 8:00am to 9:00pm, Saturday: 8:00am to 5:00pm, Sunday: 11:00am to 6:00pm.\n\n## More to Discover\n- [Australia](http://www.panpacific.com/en/insights/all-articles.html?country=australia)\n- [Perth](http://www.panpacific.com/en/insights/all-articles.html?city=perth)\n- [Business Traveller](http://www.panpacific.com/en/insights/all-articles.html?persona=business-traveller)\n- [City Walk](http://www.panpacific.com/en/insights/all-articles.html?interest=city-walk)\n- [First-Time Visitors](http://www.panpacific.com/en/insights/all-articles.html?interest=first-time-visitors)\n- [![Sydney, Melbourne or Perth: Which City to Travel to?](https://www.panpacific.com/content/dam/pphg-revamp/en/destination-insights/australia/all-cities/sydney-melbourne-or-perth-which-city-to-travel-to/T24-20-australia-where-to-travel-parkroyal-picks.jpg)](https://www.panpacific.com/en/insights/australia/australia-articles/sydney-melbourne-or-perth.html)\n[Australia](https://www.panpacific.com/en/insights/australia.html) [**Sydney, Melbourne or Perth: Which City to Travel to?**](https://www.panpacific.com/en/insights/australia/australia-articles/sydney-melbourne-or-perth.html)\n  - [Perth](http://www.panpacific.com/en/insights/all-articles.html?city=perth) [City Walk](http://www.panpacific.com/en/insights/all-articles.html?interest=city-walk)\n- [![Things To Do In Perth: Discover These Secret Spots](https://www.panpacific.com/content/dam/pphg-revamp/en/ppper/the-hotel/destination-insights/signpost/cottesloe-beach-secret-spots-perth-signpost.jpg)](https://www.panpacific.com/en/insights/australia/perth/things-to-do-in-perth.html)\n[Australia](https://www.panpacific.com/en/insights/australia.html) [**Things To Do In Perth: Discover These Secret Spots**](https://www.panpacific.com/en/insights/australia/perth/things-to-do-in-perth.html)\n  - [Perth](http://www.panpacific.com/en/insights/all-articles.html?city=perth) [Friends](http://www.panpacific.com/en/insights/all-articles.html?persona=friends) [Adventures](http://www.panpacific.com/en/insights/all-articles.html?interest=adventures)\n- [![Perth in Motion: River, Trails and City Tours](https://www.panpacific.com/content/dam/pphg-revamp/en/destination-insights/australia/perth/perth-in-motion/T24-1-perth-in-motion-parkroyal-picks.jpg)](https://www.panpacific.com/en/insights/australia/perth/perth-trails-and-tours.html)\n[Australia](https://www.panpacific.com/en/insights/australia.html) [**Perth in Motion: River, Trails and City Tours**](https://www.panpacific.com/en/insights/australia/perth/perth-trails-and-tours.html)\n  -         "markdown": "Cookie Preferences\n\nWe and our 1037 partners use cookies and other tracking technologies to improve your experience on our website. We may store and/or access information on a device and process personal data, such as your IP address and browsing data, for personalised advertising and content, advertising and content measurement, audience research and services development. Additionally, we may utilize precise geolocation data and identification through device scanning.\n\nPlease note that your choices apply across all our subdomains. Once you give consent, a floating button will appear at the bottom of your screen, allowing you to change or withdraw your consent at any time. We respect your choices and are committed to providing you with a transparent and secure browsing experience.\n\nManage PreferencesNoYes\n\nManage Cookie Preferences![](https://cdn-cookieyes.com/assets/images/close.svg)\n\nCustomise your consent preferences for Cookie Categories and advertising tracking preferences for Purposes & Features and Vendors below. You can give granular consent for each Third Party Vendor. Most vendors require explicit consent for personal data processing, while some rely on legitimate interest. However, you have the right to object to their use of legitimate interest. Additionally, please note that your preferences regarding purposes and vendors are saved in a cookie named 'euconsent' on your device and may be retained for up to 730 days to remember your choices.\n\n- Cookie Categories\n- Purposes & Features\n- Vendors\n\nCookie Categories\n\nWe use cookies to help you navigate efficiently and perform certain functions. You will find detailed information about all cookies under each consent category below.\n\nThe cookies that are categorized as \"Necessary\" are stored on your browser as they are essential for enabling the basic functionalities of the site. ... Show more\n\nNecessaryAlways Active\n\nNecessary cookies are required to enable the basic features of this site, such as providing secure log-in or adjusting your consent preferences. These cookies do not store any personally identifiable data.\n\n- Cookie\n\nrenderid\n\n- Duration\n\nsession\n\n- Description\n\nThis cookie is used for load balancing. The cookie contains the information on which server the visit is on.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\ns\\_ppn\n\n- Duration\n\n1 hour\n\n- Description\n\nThis cookie contains information about the current page and entry page to the website.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\ndpm\n\n- Duration\n\n6 months\n\n- Description\n\nThe dpm cookie, set under the Demdex domain, assigns a unique ID to each visiting user, hence allowing third-party advertisers to target these users with relevant ads.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\ndemdex\n\n- Duration\n\n6 months\n\n- Description\n\nThe demdex cookie, set under the domain demdex.net, is used by Adobe Audience Manager to help identify a unique visitor across domains.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\ndatadome\n\n- Duration\n\n1 year\n\n- Description\n\nThis is a security cookie set by Force24 to detect BOTS and malicious traffic.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\nPHPSESSID\n\n- Duration\n\nsession\n\n- Description\n\nThis cookie is native to PHP applications. The cookie stores and identifies a user's unique session ID to manage user sessions on the website. The cookie is a session cookie and will be deleted when all the browser windows are closed.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\nAWSELB\n\n- Duration\n\nsession\n\n- Description\n\nAssociated with Amazon Web Services and created by Elastic Load Balancing, AWSELB cookie is used to manage sticky sessions across production servers.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\nwpEmojiSettingsSupports\n\n- Duration\n\nsession\n\n- Description\n\nWordPress sets this cookie when a user interacts with emojis on a WordPress site. It helps determine if the user's browser can display emojis properly.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\ncsrftoken\n\n- Duration\n\n1 year 1 month 4 days\n\n- Description\n\nThis cookie is associated with Django web development platform for python. Used to help protect the website against Cross-Site Request Forgery attacks\n\n\n- Cookie\n\neuconsent\n\n- Duration\n\n1 year\n\n- Description\n\nCookieYes sets this cookie to store data when IAB TCF is enabled.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\nttcsid\n\n- Duration\n\n3 months\n\n- Description\n\n\n\nA first-party cookie used to maintain a user session or support core website functions. Duration\n\nsession\n\n- Description\n\nDescription is currently not available.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\n\\_\\_cc\n\n- Duration\n\n1 minute\n\n- Description\n\n\n\nStores your cookie consent status to verify These cookies help provide information on metrics such as the number of visitors, bounce rate, traffic source, etc.\n\n- Cookie\n\n\\_yjsu\\_yjad\n\n- Duration\n\n1 year\n\n- Description\n\nDescription is currently not available.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\n\\_gcl\\_au\n\n- Duration\n\n3 months\n\n- Description\n\nGoogle Tag Manager sets the cookie to experiment advertisement efficiency of websites using their services.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\ns\\_ecid\n\n- Duration\n\n1 year 1 month 4 days\n\n- Description\n\nAdobe installs this cookie to allow persistent ID tracking in the 1st party state and is used as a reference ID if the AMCV cookie has expired.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\n\\_clck\n\n- Duration\n\n1 year\n\n- Description\n\nMicrosoft Clarity sets this cookie to retain the browser's Clarity User ID and settings exclusive to that website. This guarantees that actions taken during subsequent visits to the same website will be linked to the same user ID.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\n\\_clsk\n\n- Duration\n\n1 day\n\n- Description\n\nMicrosoft Clarity sets this cookie to store and consolidate a user's pageviews into a single session recording.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\n\\_fbp\n\n- Duration\n\n3 months\n\n- Description\n\nFacebook sets this cookie to display advertisements when either on Facebook or on a digital platform powered by Facebook advertising after visiting the website.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\ns\\_ppvl\n\n- Duration\n\nsession\n\n- Description\n\nAdobe Analytics sets this cookie to store information on the percentage of the page displayed to user.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\ns\\_ppv\n\n- Duration\n\nsession\n\n- Description\n\nThe cookie is used to determine the percentage of the page that has been displayed to the user.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\ns\\_cc\n\n- Duration\n\nsession\n\n- Description\n\nAdobe Analytics sets this cookie to determine whether or not cookies are enabled in the user's browser.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\n\\_ga\n\n- Duration\n\n1 year 1 month 4 days 1 minute\n\n- Description\n\nGoogle Analytics sets this cookie to calculate visitor, session and campaign data and track site usage for the site's analytics report. The cookie stores information anonymously and assigns a randomly generated number to recognise unique visitors.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\n\\_gid\n\n- Duration\n\n1 day\n\n- Description\n\nGoogle Analytics sets this cookie to store information on how visitors use a website while also creating an analytics report of the website's performance. Some of the collected data includes the number of visitors, their source, and the pages they visit anonymously.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\n\\_ga\\_\\*\n\n- Duration\n\n1 year 1 month 4 days 1 minute\n\n- Description\n\nGoogle Analytics sets this cookie to store and count page views.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\nSM\n\n- Duration\n\nsession\n\n- Description\n\nMicrosoft Clarity cookie set this cookie for synchronizing the MUID across Microsoft domains.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\nMR\n\n- Duration\n\n7 days 1 minute\n\n- Description\n\nThis cookie, set by Bing, is used to collect user information for analytics purposes.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\n\\_gat\\_gtag\\_UA\\_\\*\n\n- Duration\n\n1 minute\n\n- Description\n\nGoogle Analytics sets this cookie to store a unique user ID.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\ns\\_getNewRepeat\n\n- Duration\n\n1 month\n\n- Description\n\nThis cookie, set by Adobe Analytics, is used to determine if a visitor is new to the site or not.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\ns\\_lv\n\n- Duration\n\n1 year 1 month 4 days\n\n- Description\n\nAdobe sets this cookie to count the time elapsed between two visits made by the same visitor.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\ns\\_lv\\_s\n\n- Duration\n\n1 hour\n\n- Description\n\nThe s\\_lv\\_s cookie is set by Adobe and is used for storing the time since the users last visited the website.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\n\\_cs\\_id\n\n- Duration\n\n1 year 1 month\n\n- Description\n\nThis cookie is used to store the ContentSquare's user identifier ID. This is a persistent cookie and expires after 13 months.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\n\\_cs\\_s\n\n- Duration\n\n1 hour\n\n- Description\n\nThis cookie is used to store the number of page's viewed by a visitor within the session for ContentSquare's solution.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\n\\_hjHasCachedUserAttributes\n\n- Duration\n\nsession\n\n- Description\n\n\n\nHotjar\n\n\n- Cookie\n\nsuid\n\n- Duration\n\n1 year\n\n- Description\n\nSimpli. fi sets this cookie to store a distinct session ID.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\n\\_ppc\\_customProfileId\n\n- Duration\n\n1 year 1 month 4 days\n\n- Description\n\n\n\nDescription is currently not available.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\nIR\\_gbd\n\n- Duration\n\nsession\n\n- Description\n\nImpact Radius sets this cookie to store a unique ID which is used to identify the user's device, when they return to the websites that used the same network.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\nIR\\_\\*\n\n- Duration\n\nsession\n\n- Description\n\nImpact Radius sets this cookie to store a unique ID which is used to identify the user's device when they return to the websites that use the same network.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\nbrwsr\n\n- Duration\n\n1 year\n\n- Description\n\nThis cookie is set by the provider Impact Radius. This cookie is used for affiliate marketing.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\nIR\\_PI\n\n- Duration\n\n1 year\n\n- Description\n\nImpact Radius sets this cookie to store a unique ID which is used to identify the user's device, when they return to the websites that used the same network.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\nai\\_session\n\n- Duration\n\n1 hour\n\n- Description\n\nThis is a unique anonymous session identifier cookie set by Microsoft Application Insights software to gather statistical usage and telemetry data for apps built on the Azure cloud platform.\n\n\nPerformance\n\nPerformance cookies are used to understand and analyze the key performance indexes of the website which helps in delivering a better user experience for the visitors.\n\n- Cookie\n\nAMCV\\_\\*AdobeOrg\n\n- Duration\n\n1 year 1 month 4 days\n\n- Description\n\nAdobe-Dtm sets this cookie to find the unique user ID that recognises the user on returning visits.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\nAMCVS\\_\\*AdobeOrg\n\n- Duration\n\nsession\n\n- Description\n\nAdobe-Dtm sets this cookie to store a unique ID to identify a unique visitor.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\n\\_uetsid\n\n- Duration\n\n1 day\n\n- Description\n\nBing Ads sets this cookie to engage Cookie\n\n\\_tt\\_enable\\_cookie\n\n- Duration\n\n3 months\n\n- Description\n\nTiktok set this cookie to collect data about behaviour and activities on the website and to measure the effectiveness of the advertising.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\nsuid\\_legacy\n\n- Duration\n\n1 year\n\n- Description\n\nCollects information on user preferences and interaction with web-campaign content which is used on CRM-campaign-platforms used by website owners for promoting events or products.\n\n\n- Cookie\n\nttcsid\\_\\*\n\n- Duration\n\n3 months\n\n- Description\n\nTikTok sets this cookie as a session
